- **fe-restyle**: Use when the user asks to change the visual style of an existing project - e.g., "restyle to Neo-brutalist", "swap to a Dark / Terminal look", or provides a fresh brief / reference. - **fe-review**: Independent second-developer review of an open PR against its ticket's spec, with no shared session with the implementer. Input is the ticket (approved spec plus later revisions); output is the full gate-3-style review for the developer's own approval gate before manual QA and merge.
